#==================================================
# Unique Buildings for Flavored Companies
#==================================================
#
# Every buildable entry allows one site per country: the state that already
# has the building, or any state of a country that has none.
#
#	potential = {
#		owner = { has_company = ... }
#		OR = {
#			any_scope_building = { is_building_type = X }		# this state's site
#			owner = { NOT = { any_scope_state = { has_building = X } } }
#		}
#	}
#	possible = {
#		custom_tooltip = {
#			text = building_company_one_site_tt
#			OR = {
#				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = X }
#				owner = { NOT = { any_scope_state = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = X } } } }
#			}
#		}
#	}
#
# `has_building` is an engine lookup, so `potential`, which the build list and
# the AI construction planner ask for every state, costs almost nothing. It
# probably misses a site that is queued but not finished (vanilla's power
# plant ai_value pairs it with an is_under_construction scan), so the exact
# scan of the owner's buildings sits in `possible`, vanilla's "can it be built
# now" check (game/common/buildings/buildings.md): the rule holds whenever
# anyone queues a site, and a second state shows the building greyed out with
# the tooltip instead of not at all.
#
# History (2026-10-03, late-game script profiles): the second branch was
# NOT = { any_state = { ... } }, scanned for every state of every owner on
# each ask; five of these blocks cost 10-14 ms a tick each. The engine
# documents any_state as global (one site in the whole world), while
# scripting_best_practices.md records it behaving owner-scoped from a
# building's potential; the owner chose one site per country, written out
# here. A first rewrite kept the exact scan in `potential` behind the
# has_building reject; the 32 basic-company buildings still cost 23.5 us a
# call (2.7 s in one capture) because most owners never build them, so the
# scan moved to `possible`.

# --- Volkswagen ---
building_volkswagen_autostadt = {
	building_group = bg_company_buildings
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/historical_company_icons/german_volkswagen.dds"
	expandable = yes
	downsizeable = yes
	has_max_level = yes
	unique = no
	city_type = city
	ai_nationalization_desire = 0
	production_method_groups = {
		pmg_volkswagen_autostadt
	}
	required_construction = construction_cost_mega_high
	ownership_type = self
	can_build_private = {
		always = no
	}
	background = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/backgrounds/building_panel_bg_monuments.dds"
	potential = {
		owner = {
			has_company = company_type:company_volkswagen
		}
		OR = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_volkswagen_autostadt
			}
			owner = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_state = {
						has_building = building_volkswagen_autostadt
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = building_company_one_site_tt
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_volkswagen_autostadt
				}
				owner = {
					NOT = {
						any_scope_state = {
							any_scope_building = {
								is_building_type = building_volkswagen_autostadt
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	building_group = bg_company_buildings
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/historical_company_icons/italian_eni.dds"
	expandable = yes
	downsizeable = yes
	has_max_level = yes
	unique = no
	city_type = city
	ai_nationalization_desire = 0
	production_method_groups = {
		pmg_eni_palazzo
	}
	required_construction = construction_cost_mega_high
	ownership_type = self
	can_build_private = {
		always = no
	}
	background = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/backgrounds/building_panel_bg_monuments.dds"
	potential = {
		owner = {
			has_company = company_type:company_eni
		}
		OR = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_eni_palazzo
			}
			owner = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_state = {
						has_building = building_eni_palazzo
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = building_company_one_site_tt
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_eni_palazzo
				}
				owner = {
					NOT = {
						any_scope_state = {
							any_scope_building = {
								is_building_type = building_eni_palazzo
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				has_company = company_type:company_basic_oil
			}
		}
		OR = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_generic_tank_farm
			}
			owner = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_state = {
						has_building = building_generic_tank_farm
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	downsizeable = yes
	has_max_level = yes
	unique = no
	city_type = city
	ai_nationalization_desire = 0
	production_method_groups = {
		pmg_ajinomoto_kawasaki_plant
	}
	required_construction = construction_cost_mega_high
	ownership_type = self
	can_build_private = {
		always = no
	}
	background = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/backgrounds/building_panel_bg_monuments.dds"
	potential = {
		owner = {
			has_company = company_type:company_ajinomoto
		}
		OR = {
			any_scope_building = {
				is_building_type = building_ajinomoto_kawasaki_plant
			}
			owner = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_state = {
						has_building = building_ajinomoto_kawasaki_plant
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = building_company_one_site_tt
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_ajinomoto_kawasaki_plant
				}
				owner = {
					NOT = {
						any_scope_state = {
							any_scope_building = {
								is_building_type = building_ajinomoto_kawasaki_plant
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
